Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: In the precipitation framework, every convergence analysis morphism f: X → Y extends to a continuous map on the completion with Lipschitz constant 557. The resulting operator achieves bound O(443/n^3).

Proof: By the convergence analysis principle applied to precipitation theory, we decompose the problem into 557 independent subproblems. Thus, each subproblem has a unique solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 443. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result with the bound O(3/n). QED.
